## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T15:15:00Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 4cfadc88-3903-4282-8b1b-f772328ae754
**Question**: - Problem: stop strangers from editing or defacing other people's ads (ownership)\n- Accounts: anyone can sign up themselves, then log in\n- Visitors who are not logged in can browse the list of ads and view a single ad; creating and editing need login\n- A logged-in user can edit only the ads they created themselves; no admin role\n- Ads created before login existed become unowned and nobody can edit them\n- Success: users can sign in and out from the web UI; protected actions are refused for visitors; users cannot edit ads they don't own\n- Stakeholders: the product owner decides scope and priority; the backend and frontend teams implement and are consulted\n- Left out of the first version: password reset, email verification, social / SSO login, profile editing and account deletion. The first version is sign-up, log in and log out only\n- Why now: a planned launch, or opening the board to more people\n- Communication: updates in a shared channel or tracker (e.g. Slack, Jira) as work moves\n- Product owner: Maksym Tsybulskyi\n\nDoes this all look correct before I generate the artifact?
**Reply**: Looks correct

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T15:42:10Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 4cfadc88-3903-4282-8b1b-f772328ae754
**Question**: - Sign up and log in with email address + password\n- Password: at least 8 characters, no other rules\n- A login lasts until the browser is closed, or until the user logs out\n- Accounts and ads may be wiped when the backend restarts; no durable storage in this first version\n- After repeated wrong passwords the account is locked temporarily (e.g. 15 minutes after 5 failed attempts)\n- Every ad shows its poster's display name, asked for at sign-up; email addresses are never shown\n- When live, the frontend and API are served from the same web address (API under /api)\n- The database console stays open as it is today; this first version runs only in trusted environments, and you accept that the console can bypass ad ownership\n\nDoes this all look correct before I generate the requirements artifact?
**Reply**: Looks correct

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T15:55:20Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 4cfadc88-3903-4282-8b1b-f772328ae754
**Question**: - Logins are server-side sessions identified by an HttpOnly session cookie; log-out invalidates the session immediately\n- Two backend building blocks: Accounts (sign-up, user records, password check, lockout) and Authentication (log-in/log-out, session, identifying the caller)\n- An ad stores its owner's account id; the poster's display name is looked up from Accounts when ads are returned\n- Frontend: no routing library; log-in and sign-up appear as panels on the existing single screen, with a shared "current user" state\n- The "only the owner may edit" rule lives in the ads business logic, which compares the ad's owner with the caller and never takes an owner from the request body\n\nDoes this all look correct before I generate the artifact?
**Reply**: Looks correct

**Question**: Decomposition plan:\n- Strategy: one unit per repository / team\n- U1 `backend-auth` (kind: service) in bulletin-board-backend: Accounts, Authentication, and ownership in Ads (M)\n- U2 `frontend-login` (kind: ui) in bulletin-board-frontend: ApiClient, CurrentUser, AuthPanels, AdBoard (M)\n- Dependencies: none between the units; both build against the API contract from the next stage and are integrated at the end\n- Deployment: each unit deploys with its own repository, served from one web address\n\nApprove this plan?
**Reply**: Approve Plan
